# Skills · R · part 54

841 entries, reflect-corpus to refresh-hardware-bom. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reflect-corpus**: Reflect on the whole corpus — gather the window the event's `since` opens with one bounded listing, write a changelog entry on each document there is something to say about, and post exactly one digest thread, empty windows included. Invoked by the orchestrate skill for workspace.reflect events. ([trupin/corpus](https://github.com/trupin/corpus/tree/HEAD/assets/workspace/claude/skills/reflect-corpus))
- **reflect-cycle**: Guided router for the reflection half of the development cycle — folding what the build process learned back into durable docs, plans, and priorities. Runs learn → update-plan → decision-audit → review-priorities as a sequence of phases, but STOPS between each phase because every one contains a human gate (a grill-me interview and/or its own PR). Use after a batch of build/bugfix work, when you want to consolidate learnings and re-rank what's next, or say "close the loop" / "reflect on recent work". ([CERTCC/Vultron](https://github.com/CERTCC/Vultron/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/reflect-cycle) · ★22)
- **reflect-decisions**: 生成した画面を確認して人が決めたこと（推測への答え、未決への答え、直してほしいこと）を、アプリの md・画面の要件ファイル・ルール資料に書き足す。書く前に必ず内容の承認を取る。ふつうは Skill implement-ui が、画面を採用する・しないと決まったときにこの手順を呼ぶ。「決めたことを残して」「推測を戻して」「次から迷わないように書いておいて」「レポートの内容をルールに戻したい」と言われたときにも使う。 ([Gaji-Labo/ct-schedule](https://github.com/Gaji-Labo/ct-schedule/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reflect-decisions))
- **reflect-design**: Use this skill to generate well-branded interfaces and assets for Reflect (reflect.app, the minimalist networked note-taking app), either for production or throwaway prototypes/mocks/etc. Contains essential design guidelines, colors, type, fonts, assets, and UI kit components for prototyping. ([team-reflect/reflect-open](https://github.com/team-reflect/reflect-open/tree/HEAD/design-system) · ★1.6k · 2 copies)

- **reflect-dont-hand-write**: Use when writing or editing C++ in this repo that maps enum values to names, names to enum values, parses command-line flags, or builds or reads a JSON object field by field. Trigger phrases: "switch returning strings", "name table", "enum to string", "parse flags", "serialize struct", "add a field", "add a command", "add an enumerator". ([filipsajdak/llmgate](https://github.com/filipsajdak/llmgate/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/reflect-dont-hand-write))

Replaces text that repeats a type definition with code derived from the type by std::meta reflection. Trigger phrases - switch returning strings, enum to string, name table, string to enum, parse flags, serialize struct, add a field, add an enumerator, handler table, route table, GCC 16 reflection. ([wrocpp/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/wrocpp/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/cpp26-guard/skills/reflect-dont-hand-write))

- **reflection-method-find**: Find C# methods across every loaded assembly by name / declaring-type / parameters — including private methods. The Godot analog of Unity's 'reflection-method-find'. Returns serialized MethodData entries usable as schemas for 'reflection-method-call'. Match levels (apply to typeName / MethodName / Parameters): - typeNameMatchLevel / methodNameMatchLevel (default 1 = contains-ignoring-case): 0 ignore filter, 1 contains-ic, 2 contains-cs, 3 starts-with-ic, 4 starts-with-cs, 5 equals-ic, 6 equals-cs. - parametersMatchLevel (default 0 = ignore filter): 0 ignore, 1 count matches, 2 equals.
